# sts2.xml member docs for key namespaces (extracted from game ship file)

T:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.AutoSlay.AutoSlayConfig
    Configuration constants for AutoSlay timeouts and settings.
F:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.AutoSlay.AutoSlayConfig.runTimeout
    Maximum time for a complete run.
F:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.AutoSlay.AutoSlayConfig.defaultRoomTimeout
    Default timeout for room handlers.
F:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.AutoSlay.AutoSlayConfig.defaultScreenTimeout
    Default timeout for screen handlers.
F:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.AutoSlay.AutoSlayConfig.gameInitTimeout
    Timeout for waiting for game instance to initialize.
F:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.AutoSlay.AutoSlayConfig.runStateTimeout
    Timeout for waiting for run state to initialize.
F:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.AutoSlay.AutoSlayConfig.nodeWaitTimeout
    Timeout for waiting for nodes to appear.
F:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.AutoSlay.AutoSlayConfig.mapScreenTimeout
    Timeout for waiting for map screen.
F:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.AutoSlay.AutoSlayConfig.pollingInterval
    Polling interval for condition checks.
F:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.AutoSlay.AutoSlayConfig.buttonClickDelay
    Delay between button interactions.
F:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.AutoSlay.AutoSlayConfig.maxFloor
    Maximum floor to play to (floor 49 is the final boss).
F:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.AutoSlay.AutoSlayConfig.watchdogTimeout
    Watchdog timeout. If no progress for this long, dump state and fail.
F:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.AutoSlay.AutoSlayConfig.watchdogLogInterval
    Interval for periodic state logging when stuck detection is active.
T:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.AutoSlay.AutoSlayer
    Main orchestrator for AutoSlay. Runs the game automatically for smoke testing.
P:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.AutoSlay.AutoSlayer.IsActive
    Static flag indicating if AutoSlay is currently running.
P:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.AutoSlay.AutoSlayer.CurrentWatchdog
    Gets the current watchdog instance (for WaitHelper integration).
M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.AutoSlay.AutoSlayer.Start(System.String,System.String)
    Starts an AutoSlay run with the given seed.
M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.AutoSlay.AutoSlayer.Stop
    Stops the current AutoSlay run.
M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.AutoSlay.AutoSlayer.GetCurrentScreen``1
    Gets the current overlay screen cast to the expected type.
T:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.AutoSlay.AutoSlayLog
    Structured logging for AutoSlay with consistent prefixes for easy filtering. Writes to both the standard Godot log and a dedicated autoslay.log file.
M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.AutoSlay.AutoSlayLog.OpenLogFile(System.String)
    Opens the log file for writing. Call at start of autoslay run.
M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.AutoSlay.AutoSlayLog.CloseLogFile
    Closes the log file. Call at end of autoslay run.
T:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.AutoSlay.AutoSlayTimeoutException
    Exception thrown when AutoSlay times out waiting for a condition or progress. Filtered from Sentry since these are expected during automated testing.
T:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.AutoSlay.Handlers.IHandler
    Base interface for all AutoSlay handlers.
P:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.AutoSlay.Handlers.IHandler.Timeout
    Timeout for this handler.
M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.AutoSlay.Handlers.IHandler.HandleAsync(MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Random.Rng,System.Threading.CancellationToken)
    Executes the handler logic.
T:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.AutoSlay.Handlers.IRoomHandler
    Handler for a specific room type.
P:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.AutoSlay.Handlers.IRoomHandler.HandledTypes
    The room types this handler can handle.
T:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.AutoSlay.Handlers.IScreenHandler
    Handler for a specific overlay screen type.
P:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.AutoSlay.Handlers.IScreenHandler.ScreenType
    The screen type this handler can handle.
T:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.AutoSlay.Handlers.Rooms.CombatRoomHandler
    Handles combat rooms (Monster, Elite, Boss). Applies massive defensive buffs and plays all cards each turn.
T:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.AutoSlay.Handlers.Rooms.EventRoomHandler
    Handles event rooms by selecting random choices. Some events trigger combat mid-event (e.g., DenseVegetation's "Rest" option). This handler detects and handles those combats before returning to the event.
M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.AutoSlay.Handlers.Rooms.EventRoomHandler.WaitForEventOptions(Godot.Node,System.Threading.CancellationToken)
    Waits for event options to become available.
M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.AutoSlay.Handlers.Rooms.EventRoomHandler.HandleFakeMerchantEvent(MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Nodes.Events.Custom.NFakeMerchant,System.Threading.CancellationToken)
    Handles the FakeMerchant custom event. This event uses NProceedButton instead of NEventOptionButton. We simply click the proceed button to leave.
M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.AutoSlay.Handlers.Rooms.EventRoomHandler.HandleAncientEventDialogue(MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Nodes.Events.NAncientEventLayout,System.Threading.CancellationToken)
    Handles Ancient events which require clicking through dialogue before options appear.
T:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.AutoSlay.Handlers.Rooms.RestSiteRoomHandler
    Handles rest site rooms by selecting a random rest option.
T:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.AutoSlay.Handlers.Rooms.ShopRoomHandler
    Handles shop rooms by attempting to buy every affordable item.
T:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.AutoSlay.Handlers.Rooms.TreasureRoomHandler
    Handles treasure rooms by opening the chest, picking relics, and proceeding.
T:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.AutoSlay.Handlers.Rooms.VictoryRoomHandler
    Handles post-combat victory rooms where the proceed button is displayed.
T:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.AutoSlay.Handlers.Screens.CardRewardScreenHandler
    Handles the card reward selection screen after combat.
T:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.AutoSlay.Handlers.Screens.ChooseABundleScreenHandler
    Handles bundle card selection screens.
T:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.AutoSlay.Handlers.Screens.ChooseACardScreenHandler
    Handles in-combat card selection screens (e.g., from Attack Potion).
T:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.AutoSlay.Handlers.Screens.ChooseARelicScreenHandler
    Handles relic selection screens (e.g., from Jewelry Box event).
T:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.AutoSlay.Handlers.Screens.CrystalSphereScreenHandler
    Handles the Crystal Sphere minigame event screen. Uses divinations by clicking cells, then clicks proceed. Note: When divinations run out, a rewards screen opens automatically. The drain loop will handle that, then this handler runs again to click proceed.
T:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.AutoSlay.Handlers.Screens.DeckCardSelectScreenHandler
    Handles the deck card selection screen (removal, transform, etc). The screen has two confirm buttons: one for the main grid, and one in the preview container. When selecting exactly MaxSelect cards, the screen auto-shows the preview, so we need to click the preview confirm button, not the main one.
T:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.AutoSlay.Handlers.Screens.DeckEnchantScreenHandler
    Handles the deck enchant selection screen.
T:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.AutoSlay.Handlers.Screens.DeckTransformScreenHandler
    Handles the deck transform selection screen.
T:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.AutoSlay.Handlers.Screens.DeckUpgradeScreenHandler
    Handles the deck upgrade selection screen.
T:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.AutoSlay.Handlers.Screens.GameOverScreenHandler
    Handles the game over screen (shown after victory or death). Clicks through the summary to return to main menu.
T:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.AutoSlay.Handlers.Screens.MapScreenHandler
    Handles map screen navigation between rooms.
T:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.AutoSlay.Handlers.Screens.RewardsScreenHandler
    Handles the rewards screen after combat.
T:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.AutoSlay.Handlers.Screens.SimpleCardSelectScreenHandler
    Handles simple card selection screens. Some screens require multiple card selections and/or manual confirmation.
T:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.AutoSlay.Helpers.AutoSlayCardSelector
    Handles automatic card selection during AutoSlay runs. Randomly selects cards when the game would normally show a selection UI.
M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.AutoSlay.Helpers.AutoSlayCardSelector.GetSelectedCards(System.Collections.Generic.IEnumerable{MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Models.CardModel},System.Int32,System.Int32)
    Randomly selects cards from the available options based on the selector preferences.
M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.AutoSlay.Helpers.AutoSlayCardSelector.GetSelectedCardReward(System.Collections.Generic.IReadOnlyList{MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Entities.Cards.CardCreationResult},System.Collections.Generic.IReadOnlyList{MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Entities.CardRewardAlternatives.CardRewardAlternative})
    Randomly selects a card reward from the available options.
T:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.AutoSlay.Helpers.UiHelper
    UI interaction utilities for AutoSlay.
M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.AutoSlay.Helpers.UiHelper.Click(MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Nodes.GodotExtensions.NClickableControl,System.Int32)
    Clicks a clickable control by directly emitting the Released signal. This bypasses hover/focus/pause checks that can fail in headless/automated testing.

    Finds all nodes of a specific type under a parent node recursively.
M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.AutoSlay.Helpers.UiHelper.FindFirst``1(Godot.Node)
    Finds the first node of a specific type under a parent node.

    Async waiting utilities for AutoSlay.

    Waits until a condition becomes true, with timeout. Also checks the watchdog periodically to detect stuck states.
M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.AutoSlay.Helpers.WaitHelper.ForNode``1(Godot.Node,System.String,System.Threading.CancellationToken,System.Nullable{System.TimeSpan})

    Waits for a task to complete while periodically checking the watchdog.
M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.AutoSlay.Helpers.WaitHelper.WithTimeout(System.Func{System.Threading.CancellationToken,System.Threading.Tasks.Task},System.TimeSpan,System.Threading.CancellationToken)
    Runs a task with a timeout, combining with an existing cancellation token.
T:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.AutoSlay.Helpers.Watchdog
    Watchdog that detects when AutoSlay gets stuck and dumps diagnostic state.
M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.AutoSlay.Helpers.Watchdog.Reset(System.String)
    Resets the watchdog timer. Call this whenever meaningful progress is made.
M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.AutoSlay.Helpers.Watchdog.Check
    Checks if the watchdog has timed out. Call this periodically (e.g., in WaitHelper). Throws TimeoutException if no progress for too long.
M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.AutoSlay.Helpers.Watchdog.DumpState
    Dumps the current game state for debugging.
T:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.AutoSlay.MemoryProfiler
    Captures memory and resource snapshots during AutoSlay runs, logging deltas from a baseline to detect memory/resource leaks.
E:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.CombatManager.CombatSetUp
    Fired after combat is set up. Note that this happens a little bit before combat actually begins.
E:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.CombatManager.CombatEnded
    Fired when combat ends.
E:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.CombatManager.CombatWon
    Fired when combat is won.
E:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.CombatManager.CreaturesChanged
    Fired whenever the arrangement of creatures in the combat changes. Specifically, when: * A creature is added. * A creature is removed. * A creature's position changes.
E:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.CombatManager.TurnStarted
    Fired whenever a new turn starts.
E:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.CombatManager.TurnEnded
    Fired whenever a turn ends.
E:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.CombatManager.PlayerEndedTurn
    Fired whenever a player ends their turn. Remember that, in multiplayer, this is not the same as switching to the enemy's turn.
E:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.CombatManager.PlayerUnendedTurn
    Fired whenever a player un-does the end of their turn.
E:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.CombatManager.AboutToSwitchToEnemyTurn
    Fired when all players have fully committed to ending turn and all player actions are done (including end of turn hooks like Well-Laid Plans), but before the player hand flush.
E:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.CombatManager.PlayerActionsDisabledChanged
    Fired when the local player's actions become disabled or enabled.
F:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.CombatManager._inPlayerTurnSetup
    True while <see cref="M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.CombatManager.StartTurn(System.Func{System.Threading.Tasks.Task})"/> is setting up the player turn but has not yet moved to the Play phase. Guarded by <see cref="F:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.CombatManager._playerReadyLock"/>.
F:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.CombatManager._deferredEndTurnTransition
    If a card ends the turn while <see cref="F:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.CombatManager._inPlayerTurnSetup"/> is true (e.g. Void Form auto-played by Whispering Earring during the AutoPrePlay phase), the end-of-turn transition is stored in this field and run only once <see cref="M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.CombatManager.StartTurn(System.Func{System.Threading.Tasks.Task})"/> has caused us to move to the 
P:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.CombatManager.DebugForcedTopCardOnNextShuffle
    WARNING: ONLY USE THIS IN TESTS! See <see cref="M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.CombatManager.DebugForceTopCardOnNextShuffle(MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Models.CardModel)"/>.
M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.CombatManager.DebugOnlyGetState
    THIS IS TEMPORARY AND SHOULD ONLY BE USED IN TESTS
F:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.CombatManager._playerActionsDisabled
    Set to true when the player should not be able to interact with their hand or any potions.
P:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.CombatManager.PlayersTakingExtraTurn
    The list of players in the current turn that are taking an extra turn. Normally empty; only non-empty if there are players that used extra-turn-taking effects like <see cref="T:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Models.Relics.PaelsEye"/>. Returns a snapshot copy for thread safety.
M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.CombatManager.SetPhaseForAllPlayers(MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.PlayerTurnPhase)
    Sets <see cref="P:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Entities.Players.PlayerCombatState.Phase"/> to the same value for all players.
P:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.CombatManager.IsEnemyTurnStarted
    True when the enemy turn has started (TurnStarted has fired for the enemy side). Set right before TurnStarted fires for enemy turns, cleared when switching to player turn.
P:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.CombatManager.EndingPlayerTurnPhaseTwo
    Set to true in the time between when all players are ready to begin the enemy turn and when the enemy turn begins.
P:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.CombatManager.EndingPlayerTurnPhaseOne
    Set to true in the time during phase one of the end of the player's turn.
P:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.CombatManager.IsInProgress
    Is the combat currently in progress? True when the combat is done being initialized and has fully started. False when: * The combat is first being initialized. * The combat is ending (the last monster has been killed). * We're in a non-combat room.
P:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.CombatManager.IsStarting
    Is a new combat currently being set up? True from the start of <see cref="M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.CombatManager.SetUpCombat(MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.CombatState)"/> until <see cref="P:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.CombatManager.IsInProgress"/> flips true in <see cref="M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.CombatManager.StartCombatInternal"/>. During this window <see cref="P:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.Combat
M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.CombatManager.IsExecutingCardOrPotionEffect(MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Entities.Players.Player)
    True while a <see cref="M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Models.CardModel.OnPlay(MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.GameActions.Multiplayer.PlayerChoiceContext,MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Entities.Cards.CardPlay)"/> or a <see cref="M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Models.PotionModel.OnUse(MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.GameActions.Multiplayer.PlayerChoiceContext,MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Entities.Creatures.Creature)"/> effect body is currently executing for <param
M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.CombatManager.BeginCardOrPotionEffect(MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Entities.Players.Player)
    Marks the start of a <see cref="M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Models.CardModel.OnPlay(MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.GameActions.Multiplayer.PlayerChoiceContext,MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Entities.Cards.CardPlay)"/> or <see cref="M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Models.PotionModel.OnUse(MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.GameActions.Multiplayer.PlayerChoiceContext,MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Entities.Creatures.Creature)"/> effect body for <paramref name="player"

    Marks the end of a <see cref="M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Models.CardModel.OnPlay(MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.GameActions.Multiplayer.PlayerChoiceContext,MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Entities.Cards.CardPlay)"/> or <see cref="M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Models.PotionModel.OnUse(MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.GameActions.Multiplayer.PlayerChoiceContext,MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Entities.Creatures.Creature)"/> effect body for <paramref name="player"/>
P:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.CombatManager.IsAboutToLose
    Is combat about to end due to player death? True when LoseCombat() has been called but the loss hasn't been processed yet. This allows effects to bail out early while still letting the current action complete.
P:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.CombatManager.IsEnding
    Is the combat in the process of ending (but still in progress)? True when combat is in progress but all the enemies are dead, and there is nothing stopping combat from ending (e.g. Phrog Parasite spawning in new enemies). Also true when a pending loss is waiting to be processed. False when * Combat is in progress and 1+ primary enemies are still alive. * Combat is not in progress.
P:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.CombatManager.IsOverOrEnding
    Has this combat ended (or is it in the process of ending)? When you want to skip/cancel an effect because combat is not in progress, you should usually use this instead of <see cref="P:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.CombatManager.IsEnding"/> or !<see cref="P:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.CombatManager.IsInProgress"/>, because they can return unexpected results at certain boundary points.
M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.CombatManager.RunAutoPrePlayPhase(MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.GameActions.Multiplayer.HookPlayerChoiceContext,System.Threading.Tasks.Task,MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Entities.Players.Player)
    Awaits the player's setup task, then runs the auto-pre-play hooks, transitioning the player's phase from <see cref="F:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.PlayerTurnPhase.Start"/> -> <see cref="F:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.PlayerTurnPhase.AutoPrePlay"/> -> <see cref="F:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.PlayerTurnPhase.Play"/>. The setup await ensures a player whose setup is paused (making a <see cref="T:MegaCrit.Sts2
M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.CombatManager.SetupPlayerTurn(MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Entities.Players.Player,MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.GameActions.Multiplayer.HookPlayerChoiceContext)
    Sets up a player's turn by resetting energy, drawing cards, and firing start-of-turn hooks. If the player's turn start executes a player choice (e.g. Mayhem plays Cosmic Indifference), then the entire sequence is paused for this player. However, other players' turn start sequences may continue, and they may play cards while this is occuring.
M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.CombatManager.SetReadyToEndTurn(MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Entities.Players.Player,System.Boolean,System.Func{System.Threading.Tasks.Task})
    Called in EndPlayerTurnAction to indicate that the player is ready to execute end-of-turn events.
M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.CombatManager.ReleaseDeferredEndTurnTransitionIfNeeded
    Runs any end-of-turn transition that was deferred while the player turn was being set up (see <see cref="M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.CombatManager.SetReadyToEndTurn(MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Entities.Players.Player,System.Boolean,System.Func{System.Threading.Tasks.Task})"/>). Must be called on every exit path of the player-turn setup in <see cref="M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.CombatManager.StartTurn(Syste
M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.CombatManager.OnEndedTurnLocally
    Call this when the end turn button is pressed to disable local player actions until the start of the next turn. In multiplayer, this prevents the player from playing cards after they have ended turn. In both SP and MP, this prevents the player from playing cards before the AfterTurnStart hook has run. It's important that we do this when the end turn button is pressed, instead of when the EndTurnAc
M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.CombatManager.SetReadyToBeginEnemyTurn(MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Entities.Players.Player,System.Func{System.Threading.Tasks.Task})
    Called in ReadyToBeginEnemyTurnAction to indicate that the player is ready to switch to the monster turn (or extra player turn, if necessary). Note that this is called automatically, and is not player-driven.

M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.CombatManager.AfterCreatureAdded(MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Entities.Creatures.Creature)
    Called after both the Creature has been added to the room _and_ the NCreature is spawned.
M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.CombatManager.CheckForEmptyHand(MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.GameActions.Multiplayer.PlayerChoiceContext,MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Entities.Players.Player)
    Check for the player's hand to be empty and run the appropriate hooks if it is. We can't just do this check every time the hand size changes, because sometimes we're in the middle of a sequence of effects and we want to wait to check until they're all done. For example, if we have <see cref="T:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Models.Relics.UnceasingTop"/> and the last card in our hand is <see cref="T:MegaCrit.S
M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.CombatManager.Reset(System.Boolean)
    Reset the combat manager to prepare for the next combat.
M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.CombatManager.LoseCombat
    Marks combat as pending loss. The actual loss processing happens at the next safe point (in CheckWinCondition) to avoid race conditions where effects try to run after IsInProgress is false.
M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.CombatManager.ProcessPendingLoss
    Processes a pending combat loss. Called from CheckWinCondition at safe points.
M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.CombatManager.EndCombatInternal
    DO NOT CALL THIS unless you're in this class or ModelTest.
M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.CombatManager.EndPlayerTurnPhaseOneInternal
    DO NOT CALL THIS unless you're in this class or ModelTest. This calls all end-of-turn hooks that could require player choices to be made.
M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.CombatManager.DoTurnEnd(MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Entities.Players.Player,MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.GameActions.Multiplayer.PlayerChoiceContext)
    Executes turn end hooks for a player. If player choice occurs during this method, it uses the passed choice context. This way, each player's turn end runs independently of all others.
M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.CombatManager.EndPlayerTurnPhaseTwoInternal
    DO NOT CALL THIS unless you're in this class or ModelTest. This does all the player state cleanup for the end of their turn. It must not call any hooks that might cause player choices to occur.
M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.CombatManager.SwitchFromPlayerToEnemySide(System.Func{System.Threading.Tasks.Task})
    DO NOT CALL THIS unless you're in this class or ModelTest. This switches from the player side to the enemy side, handling extra player turns if necessary.
M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.CombatManager.Pause
    Pause combat.
M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.CombatManager.Unpause
    Un-pause combat.
M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.CombatManager.IsPartOfPlayerTurn(MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Entities.Players.Player)
    Returns true if the passed player is taking part in the current player turn. Returns false if some player is taking an extra turn, and it's not us. If it is not the player turn, then this returns false.
M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.CombatManager.DebugForceTopCardOnNextShuffle(MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Models.CardModel)
    WARNING: ONLY CALL THIS IN TESTS! Force the specified card to be moved to the top of the next shuffle. Useful for tests for shuffle tests where the first card drawn afterwards matters.
M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.CombatManager.DebugClearForcedTopCardOnNextShuffle
    WARNING: ONLY CALL THIS IN TESTS! Clear the forced specified card to be moved to the top of the next shuffle. Useful for tests for shuffle tests where the first card drawn afterwards matters.
T:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.CombatState
    An object containing all of the state that represents the current combat. Some state details are derived from higher-level objects. For example: * All players in the combat state are derived by checking the Player property of all the creatures. * All cards in the combat state are derived by checking the combat piles of all those players.
P:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.CombatState.RunState
    The state of the run that this combat exists in. Will be <see cref="P:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.CombatState.RunState"/> in gameplay and <see cref="T:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Runs.NullRunState"/> in some test/debug scenarios.
E:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.CombatState.CreaturesChanged
    Fired whenever the arrangement of creatures in the combat changes. Specifically, when: * A creature is added. * A creature is removed. * A creature's index changes.
F:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.CombatState._nextCreatureId
    This is the ID that will be assigned to the next spawned creature's CombatId field. If we receive an action targeting a creature with an ID less than this one, then it must either be in the creature list or it had died at some point in the past. If we receive an action targeting a creature with an ID greater than or equal to this one, then it has yet to spawn (or there is some other error).
P:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.CombatState.Allies
    Get all creatures on the Allies side.
P:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.CombatState.Enemies
    Get all creatures on the Enemies side.
P:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.CombatState.Creatures
    Get all creatures in the combat on all sides.
P:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.CombatState.PlayerCreatures
    Get all the player creatures in the combat.
P:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.CombatState.Players
    Get all players in the combat.
P:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.CombatState.Modifiers
    List of custom modifiers applied to this combat, usually via daily or custom runs.
P:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.CombatState.BadgeModels
    List badge models, used when unlocking badges.
P:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.CombatState.MultiplayerScalingModel
    The model used to scale various things (block, power application) in multiplayer.
P:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.CombatState.RoundNumber
    The round of combat that we're on in this combat. A "round" encompasses both the player's turn and the enemy's turn. This starts at 1, so it should never be 0.
P:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.CombatState.CurrentSide
    The side that is active in this combat. A round starts with the player side being active, then switches to the enemy side after all players have ended their turn. When the enemy turn ends, the current side changes back to the player side, and the round number is incremented.
P:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.CombatState.EscapedCreatures
    A list of creatures that escaped (were removed without dying) during the encounter. Used when rewards are given.
F:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.CombatState._allCards
    All cards that have been created within this state. This allows us to keep track of "floating" cards that have not been added to any piles (like fake cards in upgrade previews).
M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.CombatState.CreateCard``1(MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Entities.Players.Player)
    See <see cref="M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Runs.ICardScope.CreateCard``1(MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Entities.Players.Player)"/>.
M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.CombatState.CreateCard(MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Models.CardModel,MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Entities.Players.Player)
    See <see cref="M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Runs.ICardScope.CreateCard(MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Models.CardModel,MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Entities.Players.Player)"/>.
M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.CombatState.CloneCard(MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Models.CardModel)
    WARNING: If you're specifically intending to create a clone in combat for an effect like <see cref="T:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Models.Cards.DualWield"/>, you should use <see cref="M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Models.CardModel.CreateClone"/> instead. See <see cref="M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Runs.ICardScope.CloneCard(MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Models.CardModel)"/>.

    See <see cref="M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Runs.ICardScope.AddCard(MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Models.CardModel,MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Entities.Players.Player)"/>.

M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.CombatState.CreateCreature(MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Models.MonsterModel,MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.CombatSide,System.String)
    Creates a new creature from a monster in this combat state. After you create the creature, you should call AddCreature to add it to combat. This can be done immediately or after some time.
M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.CombatState.AttachCreature(MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Entities.Creatures.Creature)
    Attaches an existing creature to this combat state without adding it to the combat. This sets up the creature to be added to combat without actually putting it into the combat (i.e. it's not targeted by cards that hit all enemies, powers don't proc on it, etc). For players and almost all monsters, this is called just before adding their creature to the combat. Currently, the Doormaker is the only 
M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.CombatState.CreatureEscaped(MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Entities.Creatures.Creature)
    Call this to remove a creature that escaped rather than dying.

    Removes the creature from combat.

    Get the creature with the specified combat ID. Null if not found.

    Get the creature with the specified combat ID. If the creature doesn't exist, keep checking for a while before timing out and returning null.

    Get all the creatures on the specified side.
P:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.CombatState.CreaturesOnCurrentSide
    Get all the creatures on the currently-active side.
M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.CombatState.GetOpponentsOf(MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Entities.Creatures.Creature)
    Get all opponents of a creature.
M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.CombatState.GetTeammatesOf(MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Entities.Creatures.Creature)
    Get all teammates of a creature, including the creature itself.
P:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.CombatState.HittableEnemies
    Get all hittable enemies. See <see cref="P:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Entities.Creatures.Creature.IsHittable"/> for a definition. NOTE: We shouldn't add too many methods like this, this one is just extremely common because it's used for AOE and random attack targeting.
M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.CombatState.IterateHookListeners
    Get all the models that should have combat hooks called on them.
M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.CombatState.AddCreature(MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Entities.Creatures.Creature)
    Adds a creature to the combat. This should almost always be called right after CreateCreature. Until this is called, creatures cannot be targeted and their powers will not be triggered. It is separate so that creatures can be removed from combat and re-added to it later. Currently, this is only used in the Doormaker combat.
E:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.CombatStateTracker.CombatStateChanged
    Fired whenever any part of the combat's state changes (card changes piles, power applied/removed, damage dealt, block gained/lost, etc.).
M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.CombatStateTracker.NotifyCombatStateChanged(System.String)
    Notify the world that something in the combat state changed.
T:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.History.CombatHistory
    A history of most events that have happened in combat. Each entry should be logged immediately after the event occurs, but before its AfterX hook is executed. For example, a CardPlayedEntry is logged immediately after the card is played, but before AfterCardPlayed is executed.
P:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.History.CombatHistoryEntry.Actor
    Who performed this action? Even if this was a player-specific action (like drawing a card or gaining energy), this will be the player's creature. Null if the action was not performed by any creature, like when a player loses HP from Royal Poison.
P:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.History.CombatHistoryEntry.RoundNumber
    What round was it when this action occurred? See <see cref="P:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Entities.Players.PlayerCombatState.TurnNumber"/> and <see cref="P:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.ICombatState.RoundNumber"/> to understand the difference between the two.
P:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.History.CombatHistoryEntry.CurrentSide
    Which side's turn was it when this action occurred? This will not always be the same as Actor.Side. For example, if you attack a monster with Thorns, the entry for the Thorns damage will have Actor.Side = Monster (since the monster is the one dealing damage back), but CurrentSide will be Player (since it's the player's turn).
P:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.History.CombatHistoryEntry.History
    The history object that this entry is in.
F:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.History.CombatHistoryEntry._playerTurnNumbers
    The turn numbers of all the players in combat when this action occurred.
M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.History.CombatHistoryEntry.HappenedThisTurn(MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.ICombatState)
    Did this action happen during the current turn?
M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.History.CombatHistoryEntry.HappenedLastPlayerTurn(MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Entities.Players.Player)
    Did this action happen during the specified player's last turn?
P:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.History.Entries.CardPlayFinishedEntry.CardPlay
    The instance of the card play that finished.
P:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.History.Entries.CardPlayFinishedEntry.WasEthereal
    Tracks if the card was Ethereal at the time it was played. This is a HACK in order to make <see cref="T:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Models.Cards.BansheesCry"/> work. If we end up with more WasX properties like this, we should add some sort of snapshot mechanism so that <see cref="P:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.History.Entries.CardPlayFinishedEntry.CardPlay"/> represents the state of the card at the time it wa
P:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.History.Entries.CardPlayStartedEntry.CardPlay
    The instance of the card play that started.

A "round" encompasses both the player's turn (or turns if a player takes an extra turn) and the enemy's turn. This starts at 1, so it should never be 0.

M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.ICombatState.IsLiveCombat
    Returns true if this represents a live combat. If this is false, then you should only trust that whatever object you retrieved this combat state from (like a Creature) exists, and not any other object, like other Creatures or the NCombatRoom.
T:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.PlayerTurnPhase
    Marks the phase of the player's turn in combat.
F:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.PlayerTurnPhase.None
    No phase. Used when combat is not in progress and during the enemy's turn.
F:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.PlayerTurnPhase.Start
    The start of the turn. The player is in this phase from the moment combat switches over from the enemy's turn until the player is allowed to play cards. This encompasses many things, including: - Block clear - <see cref="M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Hooks.Hook.AfterSideTurnStart(MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.ICombatState,MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.CombatSide,System.Collections.Generic.IReadOnlyList{MegaCrit.St
F:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.PlayerTurnPhase.AutoPrePlay

Examples: - <see cref="T:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Models.Relics.HistoryCourse"/> - <see c
F:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.PlayerTurnPhase.Play
    The phase of the turn where the player is allowed to manually play cards and use potions. In this phase, hooks only happen in response to player actions.
F:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.PlayerTurnPhase.AutoPostPlay
    The phase of the turn where end-of-turn auto-play effects happen. This is when <see cref="M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Hooks.Hook.AfterAutoPostPlayPhaseEntered(MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.GameActions.Multiplayer.HookPlayerChoiceContext,MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.ICombatState,MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Entities.Players.Player)"/> is called. Examples: - <see cref="T:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Models.Cards.IAmInvincible"/> - <see cre
F:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.PlayerTurnPhase.End
    The end of the turn. The player is in this phase from the moment all players have hit "end turn" and all queued cards have finished playing until combat switches over to the enemy's turn. This encompasses many things, including: - <see cref="M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Hooks.Hook.BeforeTurnEnd(MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.ICombatState,MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.CombatSide,System.Collections.Generic.IEnumerab
T:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Commands.Builders.AttackCommand
    A "builder" command that allows you to chain method calls to build up the property of an attack. This pattern is very similar to how Godot Tweens are built up.
P:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Commands.Builders.AttackCommand.Attacker
    The creature performing this attack. It's safe to assume this is non-null once the attack is being executed.
P:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Commands.Builders.AttackCommand.ModelSource
    The model that this attack comes from, such as an attack card.
F:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Commands.Builders.AttackCommand._damagePerHit
    The amount of damage that this attack will deal on each hit. This will be ignored if <see cref="F:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Commands.Builders.AttackCommand._calculatedDamageVar"/> is set.
F:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Commands.Builders.AttackCommand._calculatedDamageVar
    Dynamic var that calculates how much damage each hit does. Used for cards that do dynamic amounts of damage based on combat state, like <see cref="T:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Models.Cards.PerfectedStrike"/>. Settings this will cause <see cref="F:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Commands.Builders.AttackCommand._damagePerHit"/> to be ignored.
F:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Commands.Builders.AttackCommand._hitCount
    The number of hits this attack should do. The actual number of hits may be modified by hooks. If you need to check the final number of hits, check <see cref="P:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Commands.Builders.AttackCommand.Results"/> after the attack has been executed.
F:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Commands.Builders.AttackCommand._sourceType
    The source of this attack. Without this, we have to glean it from some combination of Attacker and ModelSource, which can get tricky in cases like Osty attacks.
F:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Commands.Builders.AttackCommand._singleTarget
    Set for single target attacks. Is null for AOE attacks.
M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Commands.Builders.AttackCommand.GetPossibleTargets
    The creatures being targeted by this attack. For random attacks, this contains all possible targets, and for custom attacks using <see cref="T:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Commands.Builders.AttackContext"/>, this will be empty. After the attack has been executed, you can check <see cref="P:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Commands.Builders.AttackCommand.Results"/> to see which targets were actually hit.
P:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Commands.Builders.AttackCommand.DamageProps
    The ValueProps of the damage that this attack deals.
P:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Commands.Builders.AttackCommand.IsSingleTargeted
    Whether this attack is targeting a single creature.
P:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Commands.Builders.AttackCommand.IsMultiTargeted
    Whether this attack is targeting multiple creatures.
P:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Commands.Builders.AttackCommand.IsRandomlyTargeted
    Whether this attack is randomly targeted.
P:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Commands.Builders.AttackCommand.Results
    The resulting instances of damage from this attack. Each inner list is from an individual hit from this attack (so if we have a multi-attack, we have multiple lists) This will start out empty, and will be populated after <see cref="M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Commands.Builders.AttackCommand.Execute(MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.GameActions.Multiplayer.PlayerChoiceContext)"/> finishes running.
F:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Commands.Builders.AttackCommand._waitBeforeHit
    fast, standard
M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Commands.Builders.AttackCommand.#ctor(System.Decimal)
    Create a new attack command.
M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Commands.Builders.AttackCommand.#ctor(MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Localization.DynamicVars.CalculatedDamageVar)
    Create a new attack command
M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Commands.Builders.AttackCommand.FromCard(MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Models.CardModel)
    Set the attack to come from the specified card. This also automatically sets the attacker as the card's owner, and the attacker animation name/delay to the card owner's defaults.
M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Commands.Builders.AttackCommand.FromMonster(MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Models.MonsterModel)
    Set the attack to come from the specified monster.
M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Commands.Builders.AttackCommand.Targeting(MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Entities.Creatures.Creature)
    Set the attack to target the specified creature.
M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Commands.Builders.AttackCommand.TargetingAllOpponents(MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.ICombatState)
    Set the attack to target the opponents of the Attacker. Differs from TargetingAll because the list of targets is refreshed between every hit meaning that new creatures added to the combat will become valid targets
M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Commands.Builders.AttackCommand.TargetingRandomOpponents(MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.ICombatState,System.Boolean)
    Sets the attack to target random opponents of the attacker. A new random target will be chosen on each hit.
M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Commands.Builders.AttackCommand.Unpowered
    Set the attack to deal unpowered damage. WARNING: 99% of the time, when unpowered damage is dealt, it shouldn't be considered an attack (like via <see cref="T:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Models.Cards.Burn"/>). However, occasionally we want to do a real attack that skips powers (like <see cref="T:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Models.Cards.Omnislice"/>), so this is useful there.
M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Commands.Builders.AttackCommand.WithAttackerAnim(System.String,System.Single,MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Entities.Creatures.Creature)
    Set the attack to play the specified animation on the attacker.
M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Commands.Builders.AttackCommand.AfterAttackerAnim(System.Func{System.Threading.Tasks.Task})
    Logic to execute after the attacker animation plays. Good for one-offs. Note: If you find yourself calling this with the same logic in many different attacks, consider making a first-class builder method for the logic instead.

M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Commands.CardSelectCmd.Reset
    Clears all active selectors. Call this during run cleanup to prevent selectors leaked by stuck async tasks (e.g., WhisperingEarring mid-auto-play when a run ends) from affecting subsequent runs.
P:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Commands.CardSelectCmd.Selector
    The currently active automated card selector. Used by tests, AutoSlay, and gameplay effects that auto-play cards (e.g., WhisperingEarring). When set, card selection UI is bypassed and cards are selected automatically. Returns the top of the stack, or null if empty.
M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Commands.CardSelectCmd.UseSelector(MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.TestSupport.ICardSelector)
    Sets the automated card selector for the duration of the returned scope. Disposing the scope clears all selectors. Throws if a selector is already active (use PushSelector for stacking behavior).
M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Commands.CardSelectCmd.PushSelector(MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.TestSupport.ICardSelector)
    Pushes a new selector onto the stack. When disposed, the previous selector is restored. Use this when you need temporary selector behavior (e.g., WhisperingEarring's auto-play).
M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Commands.CardSelectCmd.SuspendSelectorForTest
    TEST ONLY! Temporarily pops the top of the selector stack so tests can exercise the network-synchronized card-selection branches that are otherwise bypassed when a selector is active. Returns a no-op scope if the stack is already empty.
T:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Commands.CardSelectCmd.NoOpScope
    Used by <see cref="M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Commands.CardSelectCmd.SuspendSelectorForTest"/> to conform to the normal interface when there's no card selector.
T:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Commands.CardSelectCmd.RestoreSelectorScope
    Used by <see cref="M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Commands.CardSelectCmd.SuspendSelectorForTest"/> to temporarily suspend the current card selector so it can be restored later.
M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Commands.CardSelectCmd.ReportSoftlock
    Reports a potential softlock to Sentry and logs an error. Called when a selection screen would have been shown with 0 options, which would leave the player stuck. The caller returns empty to gracefully recover, but the underlying cause is still a bug that should be investigated and fixed.
M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Commands.CardSelectCmd.FromChooseACardScreen(MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.GameActions.Multiplayer.PlayerChoiceContext,System.Collections.Generic.IReadOnlyList{MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Models.CardModel},MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Entities.Players.Player,System.Boolean)

Good for in-combat card generator effects like Attack Potion. If the player is the local player, this brings up the card selection screen. If the player is a remote player, this waits for that player to select a card.

P:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Entities.Creatures.Creature.HpDisplay
    How should this creature's health bar be displayed? This is mostly for visuals, although a few gameplay effects (like <see cref="T:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Models.Powers.HellraiserPower"/>) check it to see if the creature can currently take damage.

P:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Entities.Creatures.Creature.Pets
    Get all the pets that belong to this creature.
P:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Entities.Creatures.Creature.IsPrimaryEnemy
    Is this creature a <b>primary enemy</b>? A <b>primary enemy</b> can stay alive all alone. A <b>secondary enemy</b> will automatically die unless there's also a living primary enemy. Most enemies are primary enemies. Enemies with powers like <see cref="T:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Models.Powers.MinionPower"/> or <see cref="T:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Models.Powers.IllusionPower"/> are secondary.
P:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Entities.Creatures.Creature.IsSecondaryEnemy
    Is this creature a <b>secondary enemy</b>? See <see cref="P:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Entities.Creatures.Creature.IsPrimaryEnemy"/> for detailed definitions.
P:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Entities.Creatures.Creature.IsHittable
    Is this creature <b>hittable</b>? A <b>hittable</b> creature is alive and can be "hit" by effects. This is different from <b>targetable</b>; an un-targetable creature can still be hit by AOE effects.
P:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Entities.Creatures.Creature.CanReceivePowers
    Can this creature have powers applied to it? A creature can have powers applied to it if it's in combat and can be "hit" by effects. This is different from <b>targetable</b>; an un-targetable creature can have powers applied to it. It's also different from <b>hittable</b>; a creature is not hittable if it's dead, but dead creatures can still have powers applied to them.

T:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Entities.Creatures.DamageResult
    A class containing a bunch of meta-info about an instance of damage that was dealt to a creature. See comments on individual properties for more details and examples.

    The creature that received the damage.
P:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Entities.Creatures.DamageResult.Props

P:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Entities.Creatures.DamageResult.BlockedDamage
    The amount of damage that was blocked by the target.
P:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Entities.Creatures.DamageResult.UnblockedDamage
    The amount of damage that the target received after blocking all that it could.
P:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Entities.Creatures.DamageResult.OverkillDamage
    If the target was killed, this will be the amount of extra damage that the target received after their HP dropped to 0.
P:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Entities.Creatures.DamageResult.TotalDamage
    The total amount of damage that the target received, including both blocked and unblocked.
P:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Entities.Creatures.DamageResult.WasBlockBroken
    Whether the creature's block was broken by this instance of damage. Note: If possible, you should prefer using the AfterBlockBroken hook instead of checking this, as non-damage sources like Expose can remove block.
P:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Entities.Creatures.DamageResult.WasFullyBlocked
    Whether the entirety of this instance of damage was blocked.
P:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Entities.Creatures.DamageResult.WasTargetKilled
    Was the creature that took damage killed by it? Note: This will return true even if the creature was resurrected afterwards (Fairy in a Bottle, etc.).

    Points earned in the <see cref="T:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Models.Events.WelcomeToWongos"/> event. We can't save these immediately to SerializableProgress because, otherwise, the player would be able to exit the client, reload the event, and accumulate more points. We can't write to SerializableProgress and wait for something else to save it, because it only saves at the end of a run. So, we have to sav
P:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Entities.Players.ExtraPlayerFields.CccomboBadgeUnlocked
    Whether or not the player unlocked the CCCCombo badge.

P:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Entities.Players.ExtraPlayerFields.DebuffsApplied
    How many debuffs the player applied to enemies, aggregated among all combats.
P:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Entities.Players.Player.PlayerRng
    Player-scoped RNG set. Note that this state is not deterministic outside of combat, events and rest sites This is initialized with a seed of 0, but is updated to a real seed when added to a <see cref="P:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Entities.Players.Player.RunState"/>.
P:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Entities.Players.Player.PlayerOdds
    Player-scoped odds set. Note that this state is not deterministic outside of combat, events and rest sites. This is initialized with a seed of 0, but is updated to a real seed when added to a <see cref="P:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Entities.Players.Player.RunState"/>.
P:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Entities.Players.Player.RelicGrabBag
    Player-scoped relic grab bag. See <see cref="P:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Runs.IRunState.SharedRelicGrabBag"/> for the difference between this and the shared grab bag. Note that this state is not deterministic outside of combat, events and rest sites.
P:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Entities.Players.Player.UnlockState
    The set of unlocks the player entered the run with. This serves several purposes: - In multiplayer, this keeps track of each player's unlocks so that we generate only cards/potions/relics that the player has unlocked. - In both singleplayer and multiplayer, unlocking epochs outside of the run does not change the unlocks in a run that you load. The unlocks are saved into the run save file.
P:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Entities.Players.Player.RunState
    The state of the run that the player is in. Will usually be an instance of <see cref="P:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Entities.Players.Player.RunState"/>, but will be <see cref="T:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Runs.NullRunState"/> in some spots for testing, debugging, or one-off combat scenarios. You should never have to check for this though, as <see cref="T:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Runs.NullRunState"/> should always behave

It is used to allow hooks that prevent death to run before the player is fully considered dead. This is on Player and not Creature because monsters use a different mechanism for death prevention. Likely only called in multiplayer scenarios - players cannot revive in singleplayer (remember that death prevention is different).

M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Entities.TreasureRelicPicking.RelicPickingResult.GenerateRelicFight(System.Collections.Generic.List{MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Entities.Players.Player},MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Models.RelicModel,System.Func{MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Entities.TreasureRelicPicking.RelicPickingFightMove})
    Generates a relic fight and returns it as a relic picking result. The relic fight follows these rules: - It's a rock-paper-scissors game. - Each round, each player rolls a move using the generateMove function that is passed. - If there are not exactly two distinct moves rolled, then the round ends in a tie. - If there are two distinct moves rolled, everyone who rolled the losing move is eliminated

T:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.GameActions.EndPlayerTurnAction

T:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.GameActions.GameAction
    A GameAction is a thin wrapper around an async task that should be run in response to player input. THIS IS DIFFERENT from GameActions in STS1 (and the original Unity version of STS2). In STS1, a GameAction represented a small unit of game logic, like dealing damage, gaining block, etc. In STS2, these small units of logic are handled by Commands (see the MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Commands namespace). A G
P:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.GameActions.GameAction.ActionType
    The type of game action, which dictates cancellation and enqueuing timings. See comments in the enum for details.
E:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.GameActions.GameAction.JustBeforeFinished
    Called when the GameAction is fully run to completion, but just before we complete _completionSource. This timing is important for checksum generation.
E:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.GameActions.GameAction.AfterFinished
    Called when the GameAction is fully run to completion.
E:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.GameActions.GameAction.BeforeExecuted
    Called just before the GameAction begins execution for the first time. Not called when the GameAction is resumed.
E:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.GameActions.GameAction.BeforeCancelled
    Called when the GameAction is cancelled.
E:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.GameActions.GameAction.BeforePausedForPlayerChoice
    Called when the GameAction is paused for player choice.
E:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.GameActions.GameAction.BeforeReadyToResumeAfterPlayerChoice

E:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.GameActions.GameAction.BeforeResumedAfterPlayerChoice
    Called when the GameAction resumes execution after player choice.

T:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.GameActions.MoveToMapCoordAction
    An action enqueued by the host after all players have issued a vote at the map screen. This needs to be enqueued, instead of depending on everyone picking the same map point, because a random map point may be chosen in the event that two or more map points receive the same number of votes. We do not depend on RNGs being deterministic outside of combat.
T:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.GameActions.Multiplayer.ActionQueueSet
    Contains one action queue per player. We use multiple action queues to resolve the following scenario: - One player queues up three or more card plays, the second of which requires player choice - Their queue blocks on the player choice. The third action awaits completion of the player choice - Other players may freely queue card plays to their queues and have them executed
M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.GameActions.Multiplayer.ActionQueueSet.EnqueueWithoutSynchronizing(MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.GameActions.GameAction)
    Only use this if you really know what you're doing! Improper use can lead to state divergence. Enqueues an action directly to the owner's action queue.
M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.GameActions.Multiplayer.ActionQueueSet.IsGameActionPlayerDriven(MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.GameActions.GameAction)
    Returns true if a GameAction is player driven, false if it is emitted automatically. If this ends up being used in a few different places, it can be moved somewhere else; for now, it's obscure enough that it should only be used here.
M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.GameActions.Multiplayer.ActionQueueSet.GetReadyAction
    Returns the next action on top of a player's queue that should be executed. Selection works like this: - If any player's queue is awaiting a player choice, then that queue is skipped. - Otherwise, we return the action with the minimum ID (the first queued by the host) at the top of any player's queue. This method should not be called when an action is already being executed.
M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.GameActions.Multiplayer.ActionQueueSet.PauseActionForPlayerChoice(MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.GameActions.GameAction,MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Entities.Multiplayer.PlayerChoiceOptions)
    Pauses an action for player choice. The action will be removed from the ActionExecutor and a new action will be allowed to execute, if there are any actions that are ready. To resume the action after this is called, call RequestResumeActionAfterPlayerChoice.
M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.GameActions.Multiplayer.ActionQueueSet.BecameEmpty
    Returns a task which completes when all player queues are empty. Be careful of running this in tests that involve player choice! If actions are not resumed, the task will never complete.
M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.GameActions.Multiplayer.ActionQueueSet.PauseAllPlayerQueues
    Pauses execution of all actions on all player queues, including those that are queued after this is called. Note that this does not pause execution of the currently executing action - only those that come after it.

Manual combat actions are any GameActions that have ActionType != GameActionType.NonCombat and ActionType != GameActionType.Any, and are manually played by the player (i.e. everything except for GenericHookGameAction and ReadyToSwitchToEnemyTurnAction). Note that this does not cancel the action at the front of the queue if it is executing. This is private because the timing must be synchronized with action enqueues across peers.

M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.GameActions.Multiplayer.ActionQueueSynchronizer.RequestEnqueue(MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.GameActions.GameAction)
    Main entry point for enqueueing a GameAction. If you are the host, the GameAction is directly enqueued, and a message is sent to notify clients about the action. If you are the client, the GameAction is not enqueued. Instead, a message is sent to the host to request that the action be enqueued. The host sends back a confirmation, preserving ordering of the action queues.
M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.GameActions.Multiplayer.ActionQueueSynchronizer.RequestEnqueueHookAction(MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.GameActions.GenericHookGameAction)
    Requests that a GenericHookGameAction be enqueued. Hook game actions are a little different than normal game actions. They contain arbitrary logic that is executed as part of a hook. Since hooks are executed on all peers, we can identify the actions by an incrementing ID. If on host, the action is immediately enqueued and a message is sent to clients. If on client, a message is sent to the host, a
M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.GameActions.Multiplayer.ActionQueueSynchronizer.RequestResumeActionAfterPlayerChoice(MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.GameActions.GameAction)
    Requests that an action, paused for player choice, be resumed. If on the host, the action is immediately resumed and a message is sent to clients indicating this. If on a client, a message is sent to the host to ensure correct ordering, and the host sends a message back to confirm resumption of the action.

T:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.GameActions.Multiplayer.HookPlayerChoiceContext
    A player choice context for use in hooks that do not run as part of a GameAction. When player choice is required, a new game action is created and enqueued directly to the player's action queue. From there, flow proceeds like a normal game action. No game action is created if player choice is not requested.

    Reserves a choice ID to be passed to SyncLocalChoice or WaitForLocalChoice. If called during combat, this must be done in a deterministic context (i.e. before a GameAction is paused) so that checksums match up after action execution.     Constructor to use when deserializing a NetUsePotionAction. Should not be used in other circumstances.
T:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.GameActions.VoteForMapCoordAction
    An action enqueued at the map screen when the player picks/votes for a map coord to move to. Once all players have selected a map coord, the host decides which map coord to move to based on the votes. It then enqueues MoveToMapCoordAction which triggers entering the room. This action may be enqueued multiple times with the same origin location, and should overwrite which coord the owning player is
F:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.GameActions.VoteForMapCoordAction._player
    The player who is voting.
F:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.GameActions.VoteForMapCoordAction._source
    The location that the player is currently at while casting the vote.
F:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.GameActions.VoteForMapCoordAction._destination
    The player's vote. If null, any existing votes for the player should be cancelled.
T:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.GameActions.VoteToMoveToNextActAction
    An action enqueued at the rewards screen when the player is ready to move to the next act. Once a player receives actions from all other players indicating that they're ready to move to the next act, then the player should begin transitioning to the next act.
F:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.GameActions.VoteToMoveToNextActAction._player
    The player who is voting.

M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Hooks.Hook.IterateCombatHookListeners(MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.ICombatState)
    Iterates combat hook listeners, but yields nothing if combat is over or ending (CombatManager.IsOverOrEnding) when the dispatch begins. Most combat hooks should use this instead of <see cref="M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Combat.ICombatState.IterateHookListeners"/> directly, so a hook dispatched after combat has started ending fires for no one (for example after a Strike that Hellraiser plays automatically

T:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Hooks.HpLossHookPhase
    Represents which phase(s) of HP-loss-modification hooks should be run for a given HP loss. In CreatureCmd.Damage, the two phases bracket damage redirection (Osty), so they are invoked separately with potentially different targets. Callers that don't deal with redirection (like damage previews) can use <see cref="F:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Hooks.HpLossHookPhase.All"/> to apply both phases at once.
F:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Hooks.HpLossHookPhase.BeforeOsty
    Hooks that modify HP loss before damage is redirected from Necrobinder to Osty. Including this will cause <see cref="M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Models.AbstractModel.ModifyHpLostBeforeOsty(MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Entities.Creatures.Creature,System.Decimal,MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.ValueProps.ValueProp,MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Entities.Creatures.Creature,MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Models.CardModel)"/> and <see cref="M:MegaCrit.Sts
F:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Hooks.HpLossHookPhase.AfterOsty
    Hooks that modify HP loss after damage is redirected from Necrobinder to Osty. Including this will cause <see cref="M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Models.AbstractModel.ModifyHpLostAfterOsty(MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Entities.Creatures.Creature,System.Decimal,MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.ValueProps.ValueProp,MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Entities.Creatures.Creature,MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Models.CardModel)"/> and <see cref="M:MegaCrit.Sts2.
F:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Hooks.HpLossHookPhase.All
    Include all HP-loss-modification hooks. Use when there is no redirection step between phases (e.g. damage previews).
T:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Hooks.ModifyDamageHookType
    Represents the type(s) of ModifyDamage hooks that should be run for a given instance of damage.
F:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Hooks.ModifyDamageHookType.Additive
    Additive damage hooks from effects like <see cref="T:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Models.Powers.StrengthPower"/>. Including this will cause <see cref="M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Models.AbstractModel.ModifyDamageAdditive(MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Entities.Creatures.Creature,System.Decimal,MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.ValueProps.ValueProp,MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Entities.Creatures.Creature,MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Models.CardModel)"/> and <se
F:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Hooks.ModifyDamageHookType.Multiplicative
    Multiplicative damage hooks from effects like <see cref="T:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Models.Powers.VulnerablePower"/>. Including this will cause <see cref="M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Models.AbstractModel.ModifyDamageMultiplicative(MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Entities.Creatures.Creature,System.Decimal,MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.ValueProps.ValueProp,MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Entities.Creatures.Creature,MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Models.CardMod

    Prunes duplicate segments, then repairs any point types that fell below their target count. Since repair can create new duplicates, loops until stable (up to 3 iterations).

    After pruning removes nodes, some point types may fall below their target count. This repairs the map by replacing monsters with the missing types.

    Runs the full prune loop (up to 50 iterations) to remove duplicate segments.

T:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Map.MapPostProcessing
    These helper methods make the map more clean looking and less cluttered together which should help with some of the complaints. Some might look like there's a lot of looping, but I profiled them and all these public static methods run together take 0 ms on my computer.
M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Map.MapPostProcessing.CenterGrid(MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Map.MapPoint[0:,0:])
    Creates a copy of the given grid that is “centered” by shifting all the nodes left or right if two of the leftmost or rightmost columns are empty. The MapPoint objects are not modified; they are simply placed into different cells.
M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Map.MapPostProcessing.StraightenPaths(MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Map.MapPoint[0:,0:])

M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Map.MapPostProcessing.GetNeighborAllowedPositions(System.Int32,System.Int32)
    Returns a set of column indices allowed for a neighbor at the specified column. Allowed positions are the same column, one column to the left, and one column to the right.
M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Map.MapPostProcessing.GetAllowedPositions(MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Map.MapPoint,System.Int32)
    Computes the set of allowed column indices for a given node based on its parents' and children’s positions. For each parent or child, the node must be in the same column or one column to the left or right. The final allowed set is the intersection of these constraints.
M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Map.MapPostProcessing.SpreadAdjacentMapPoints(MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Map.MapPoint[0:,0:])
    Spreads adjacent MapPoints in the grid by moving nodes to positions that maximize the gap between neighbors, subject to connectivity constraints.

    Computes the minimal horizontal gap from a candidate column to any other node in the same row. If there are no other nodes in the row, returns int.MaxValue.

After generation, all maps are simply grids of MapPoints with connections. The serialization captures the complete

T:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Modding.IModManagerFileIo
    A file IO abstraction layer for use with ModManager. It's a little different than ISaveStore, which is scoped only to a specific directory. This class allows global access to the filesystem.
T:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Modding.Mod
    Information about a loaded mod.
F:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Modding.Mod.modSource
    Where the mod originated from.
F:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Modding.Mod.path
    Path where the mod files are located.
F:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Modding.Mod.state
    Whether the mod was loaded, and if it was not loaded, why. Since there's no way to unload mods while the game is running, this value cannot change after the initial mod initialization occurs. Even if the mod is set to disabled in SettingsSave, this value is the true source of whether or not the mod was loaded into the game.
F:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Modding.Mod.manifest
    The mod manifest.
F:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Modding.Mod.version
    The version parsed from the mod manifest. Null if the version is not present or is not a valid semantic version.
F:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Modding.Mod.assembly
    The C# assembly loaded with the mod, if any.
F:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Modding.Mod.errors
    If null, then no errors occurred while loading the mod. If this is set, then there was an error loading the mod that should be displayed.

T:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Modding.ModInitializerAttribute
    Declares a class as the main entry point for the mod. If this is present, then upon loading the mod, we'll call the method named initializerMethod within the class. Otherwise, we'll create a harmony instance for the mod and call Harmony.PatchAll.
F:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Modding.ModLoadState.Loaded
    Mod was successfully loaded.
F:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Modding.ModLoadState.Failed
    We tried to load the mod but it failed to load.
F:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Modding.ModLoadState.Disabled
    We didn't try to load the mod because the user has not enabled mod loading, or this specific mod is marked as disabled in SettingsSave.
F:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Modding.ModLoadState.DisabledDuplicate
    We didn't try to load the mod because the user has this mod loaded both via Steam and local mods directory.
F:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Modding.ModLoadState.AddedAtRuntime
    We didn't try to load the mod because it was added too late.
E:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Modding.ModManager.OnModDetected
    Called when a new mod is detected. Use this to display a warning at runtime if the user adds new mods.
E:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Modding.ModManager.OnMetricsUpload
    Used by mods to hook into the metrics upload process. This is unused by STS2 code. It is only used by mods, and is called when we would ordinarily upload metrics.
M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Modding.ModManager.Initialize(MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Modding.IModManagerFileIo,MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Modding.ModSettings,MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Debug.SemanticVersion)
    Loads all mods from the appropriate directories. This includes the "mods" directory next to the executable, as well as Steam workshop files. This should be called as early as possible in the game's initialization process so that downstream classes (ModelDb, LocManager) pick up the changes that the mods apply.
M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Modding.ModManager.SortModList(System.Collections.Generic.List{MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Modding.SettingsSaveMod})
    Sorts mods so that dependencies are loaded before the mods that depend on them (topological sort). Uses Kahn's algorithm: https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Topological_sorting#Kahn's_algorithm How it works: 1. Build a graph: count each mod's dependencies (in-degree) and track reverse edges (dependentsMap). 2. Seed a frontier queue with all mods that have zero dependencies (they're ready to load). 3. D
M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Modding.ModManager.RemoveDisabledMods
    If the user has subscribed to a mod via Steam workshop and also has it in their local directory, we never want to load the Steam mod. This is to help mod developers in development.

    Recurses through the directory and reads all the mod PCKs and DLLs in it, putting it in the _mods list. We allow mods in directories to give modders a little slack in how they organize their files. Pass the newMods list if you wish to get the mods that have been newly read.

Each range is a (min, max) pair of branch names ordered oldest to newest (see <see cref="T:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Platform.PlatformBranch"/>); an empty string is a documented open-ended bound (empty min = no lower bound, empty max = no upper bound, both

When the dotnet runtime attempts to load the DLL, it tries to strictly match the version. Usually, we don't really need it to - a lot of our APIs don't change that often. Attaching this method to the AssemblyResolve event allow

    Main entry point for this class. Generates a checksum of the full combat state for validation against peers'. On host, the checksum is saved for comparison against those received from clients. On clients, the checksum is saved and sent to the host for validation. This should be called the exact same amount of times for every peer, otherwise false positive mismatches will be generated.

M:MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Runs.RunState.RemoveStaleVisitedMapCoords(MegaCrit.Sts2.Core.Map.ActMap)
    Removes any visited map coords that don't exist in the given map. Defends against old multiplayer saves (pre-v0.99.1) that had their SavedMap stripped by a canonicalization bug, causing map regeneration with different topology. The root cause is fixed but damaged saves persist.
